{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-5","id":"msg_011CenhmMaiMvtGJkfftLxpz","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01UXNtp3chEy3vthj5Fvj4oc","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/home/llmtest/evals/20260901-162328/cahn-hilliard_claude-opus-5-cc-medium_omp_r4/cahn-hilliard/cahn_hilliard.cpp","content":"#include <algorithm>\n#include <chrono>\n#include <cmath>\n#include <cstdio>\n#include <cstdlib>\n#include <cstring>\n#include <vector>\n\n#include <omp.h>\n\n#include \"../common/results_output.hpp\"\n\n// 3D index calculation\ninline constexpr size_t idx3(const size_t x, const size_t y, const size_t z, const size_t nx, const size_t ny) noexcept {\n    return z * (nx * ny) + y * nx + x;\n}\n\n// Apply a 7-point Laplacian stencil with clamped boundary conditions over the\n// whole grid and let `body(index, value, laplacian)` produce the output value.\n//\n// This is an orphaned worksharing construct: it must be called from inside a\n// parallel region. The (z, y) plane of rows is distributed with a static\n// schedule, so every sweep (and the initialization) touches the exact same\n// memory on the same thread, which keeps pages local on NUMA systems.\ntemplate <typename Body>\nstatic inline void stencilSweep(const double* __restrict in, double* __restrict out,\n                                const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,\n                                const double dx, const double dy, const double dz,\n                                Body body) {\n    const double dx2 = dx * dx;\n    const double dy2 = dy * dy;\n    const double dz2 = dz * dz;\n    const size_t slab = nx * ny;\n\n#pragma omp for collapse(2) schedule(static) nowait\n    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {\n        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {\n            const size_t base = z * slab + y * nx;\n            const size_t zpb = ((z < nz - 1) ? z + 1 : z) * slab + y * nx;\n            const size_t znb = ((z > 0) ? z - 1 : 0) * slab + y * nx;\n            const size_t ypb = z * slab + ((y < ny - 1) ? y + 1 : y) * nx;\n            const size_t ynb = z * slab + ((y > 0) ? y - 1 : 0) * nx;\n\n            const double* __restrict cc = in + base;\n            const double* __restrict cyp = in + ypb;\n            const double* __restrict cyn = in + ynb;\n            const double* __restrict czp = in + zpb;\n            const double* __restrict czn = in + znb;\n            double* __restrict o = out + base;\n\n            // x = 0 (clamped low edge)\n            {\n                const double c0 = cc[0];\n                const double lap = (cc[(nx > 1) ? 1 : 0] + c0 - 2.0 * c0) / dx2\n                                 + (cyp[0] + cyn[0] - 2.0 * c0) / dy2\n                                 + (czp[0] + czn[0] - 2.0 * c0) / dz2;\n                o[0] = body(base, c0, lap);\n            }\n\n            // interior in x: vectorizable, no boundary checks\n            for (size_t x = 1; x + 1 < nx; ++x) {\n                const double c0 = cc[x];\n                const double lap = (cc[x + 1] + cc[x - 1] - 2.0 * c0) / dx2\n                                 + (cyp[x] + cyn[x] - 2.0 * c0) / dy2\n                                 + (czp[x] + czn[x] - 2.0 * c0) / dz2;\n                o[x] = body(base + x, c0, lap);\n            }\n\n            // x = nx - 1 (clamped high edge)\n            if (nx > 1) {\n                const size_t x = nx - 1;\n                const double c0 = cc[x];\n                const double lap = (c0 + cc[x - 1] - 2.0 * c0) / dx2\n                                 + (cyp[x] + cyn[x] - 2.0 * c0) / dy2\n                                 + (czp[x] + czn[x] - 2.0 * c0) / dz2;\n                o[x] = body(base + x, c0, lap);\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Compute chemical potential\nstatic inline void computeChemicalPotential(const double* __restrict c, double* __restrict mu,\n                                            const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,\n                                            const double dx, const double dy, const double dz,\n                                            const double gamma, const double e_AA, const double e_BB, const double e_AB) {\n    stencilSweep(c, mu, nx, ny, nz, dx, dy, dz,\n                 [=](const size_t, const double cv, const double lap) {\n                     return 4.5 * ((cv + 1.0) * e_AA + (cv - 1.0) * e_BB - 2.0 * cv * e_AB)\n                            + 3.0 * cv + cv * cv * cv - gamma * lap;\n                 });\n}\n\n// Cahn-Hilliard update step\nstatic inline void cahnHilliardUpdate(double* __restrict cnew, const double* __restrict cold,\n                                      const double* __restrict mu,\n                                      const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,\n                                      const double D, const double dt, const double dx, const double dy, const double dz) {\n    stencilSweep(mu, cnew, nx, ny, nz, dx, dy, dz,\n                 [=](const size_t idx, const double, const double lap) {\n                     return cold[idx] + dt * D * lap;\n                 });\n}\n\n// Initialize concentration field (also performs the NUMA first touch of all\n// buffers, using the same distribution as the compute sweeps).\nstatic void initializeConcentration(double* __restrict c, double* __restrict cnew, double* __restrict mu,\n                                    const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {\n    const size_t vol = nx * ny * nz;\n    const size_t slab = nx * ny;\n\n#pragma omp parallel for collapse(2) schedule(static)\n    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {\n        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {\n            const size_t base = z * slab + y * nx;\n            for (size_t        }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nbool validateResult(const std::vector<double>& c, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nx, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t ny, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nz) {\n    const size_t n = c.size();\n    const double* __restrict d = c.data();\n\n    // Check for NaN or Inf\n    int bad = 0;\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) reduction(|:bad)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i (strcmp(argv[i], \"-y\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            ny = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-z\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            nz = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {\n            printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n printf(\"Cahn-Hilliard Phase Separation Benchmark\\n\");\n    printf(\"Grid size: %zu x %zu x %zu\\n\", nx, ny, nz);\n    printf(\"Time steps: %d\\n\", iterations);\n    printf(\"OpenMP threads: Raw (uninitialized) allocation lets the parallel\n    // initialization below place each page on the NUMA node of the thread\n    // that will work on it.\n    const size_t bytes = ((gridSize * sizeof(double)) + 63) & ~static_cast<size_t>(63);\n    double* cold = static_cast<double*>(std::aligned_alloc(64, bytes));\n    double* cnew = static_cast<double*>(std::aligned_alloc(64, bytes));\n    double* mu = static_cast<double*>(std::aligned_alloc(64, bytes));\n    if (!cold || !cnew || !mu) {\n        printf(\"Allocation failed\\n\");\n        return 1;\n    }\n\n    // Initialize concentration field\n    printf(\"Initializing concentration field...\\n\");\n    initializeConcentration(cold, cnew, mu, nx, ny, nz);\n\n    // Run simulation\n    printf(\"Running Cahn-Hilliard simulation...\\n\");\n    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n\n    // One parallel region for the whole time loop: threads are forked once\n    // instead of twice per time step.\n#pragma omp parallel\n    {\n        for (int t = 0; t < iterations; ++t) {\n            // Compute chemical potential\n            computeChemicalPotential(cold, mu, nx, ny, nz, dx, dy, dz,\n                                    gamma, e_AA, e_BB, e_AB);\n#pragma omp barrier\n\n            // Update concentration\n            cahnHilliardUpdate(cnew, cold, mu, nx, ny, nz, D, dt, dx, dy, dz);\n#pragma omp barrier\n\n            // Swap buffers\n#pragma omp single\n            {\n                std::swap(cold, cnew);\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - The (z, y) plane of rows is distributed with a static\n// schedule, so every sweep (and the initialization) touches the exact same\n// memory on the same thread, which keeps pages local on NUMA systems.\ntemplate <typename Body>\nstatic inline void stencilSweep(const double* __restrict in, double* __restrict out,\n                                const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,\n                                const double dx, const double dy, const double dz,\n                                Body body) {\n    const double dx2 = dx * dx;\n    const double dy2 = dy * dy;\n    const double dz2 = dz * dz;\n    const size_t slab = nx * ny;\n\n#pragma omp for collapse(2) schedule(static) nowait\n    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {\n        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {\n            const size_t base = z * slab + y * nx;\n            const size_t zpb = ((z < nz - 1) ? z + 1 : z) * slab + y * nx;\n            const size_t znb = ((z > 0) ? z - 1 : 0) * slab + y * nx;\n            const size_t ypb = z * slab + ((y < ny - 1) ? y + 1 : y) * nx;\n            const size_t ynb = z * slab + ((y > 0) ? y - 1 : 0) * nx;\n\n            const double* __restrict cc = in + base;\n            const double* __restrict cyp = in + ypb;\n            const double* __restrict cyn = in + ynb;\n            const double* __restrict czp = in + zpb;\n            const double* double lap = (cc[(nx > 1) ? 1 : 0] + c0 - 2.0 * c0) / dx2\n                                 + (cyp[0] + cyn[0] - 2.0 * c0) / dy2\n                                 + (czp[0] + czn[0] - 2.0 * c0) / dz2;\n                o[0] = body(base, c0, lap);\n            }\n\n            // interior in x: vectorizable, no boundary checks\n            for (size_t x = 1; x + 1 < nx; ++x) {\n       + (czp[x] + czn[x] - 2.0 * c0) / dz2;\n                o[x] = body(base + x, c0, lap);\n            }\n\n            // x = nx - 1 (clamped high edge)\n            if (nx > 1) {\n c0) / dy2\n                                 + (czp[x] + czn[x] - 2.0 * c0) / dz2;\n                o[x] = body(base + x, c0, lap);\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Compute chemical The (z, y) plane of rows is distributed with a static\n// schedule, so every sweep (and the initialization) touches the exact same\n// memory on the same thread, which keeps pages local on NUMA systems.\ntemplate <typename Body>\nstatic inline void stencilSweep(const double* __restrict in, double* __restrict out,\n                                const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,\n                                const double dx, const double dy, const double dz,\n                                Body body) {\n    const double dx2 = dx * dx;\n    const double dy2 = dy * dy;\n    const double dz2 = dz * dz;\n    const size_t slab = nx * ny;\n\n#pragma omp for collapse(2) schedule(static) nowait\n    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {\n        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; * nx;\n            const size_t ypb = z * slab + ((y < ny - 1) ? y + 1 : y) * nx;\n            const size_t ynb = z * slab + ((y > 0) ? y - 1 : 0) * nx;\n\n            const double* __restrict cc = in + base;\n            const double* __restrict cyp = in + ypb;\n            const double* __restrict cyn = in + ynb;\n            const double* __restrict czp = in + zpb;\n            const double* double lap = (cc[(nx > 1) ? 1 : 0] + c0 - 2.0 * c0) / dx2\n                                 + (cyp[0] + cyn[0] - 2.0 * c0) / dy2\n                                 + (czp[0] + czn[0] - 2.0 * c0) / dz2;\n                o[0] = body(base, c0, lap);\n            }\n\n            // interior in x: vectorizable, no boundary checks\n            for (size_t x = 1; x + 1 < nx; ++x) {\n                const double c0 = cc[x];\n                const double lap = (cc[x + 1] + cc[x - 1] - 2.0 * c0) / dx2\n                                 + (cyp[x] + cyn[x] - 2.0 * c0) / dy2\n                                 + (czp[x] + czn[x] - 2.0 * c0) / dz2;\n                o[x] = body(base + x, c0, lap);\n            }\n\n            // x = nx - 1 (clamped high edge)\n            if (nx > 1) {\n                const size_t x = nx - 1;\n                const double c0 = cc[x];\n                const double lap = (c0 + cc[x - 1] - 2.0 * c0) / dx2\n                                 + (cyp[x] + cyn[x] - 2.0 * c0) / dy2\n                                 + (czp[x] + czn[x] - 2.0 * c0) / dz2;\n                o[x] = body(base + x, c0, lap);\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Compute chemical potential\nstatic inline void computeChemicalPotential(const double* __restrict c, double* __restrict mu,\n                                            const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,\n                                            const double dx, const double dy, const double dz,\n                                            const double gamma, const double e_AA, const double e_BB, const double e_AB) {\n    stencilSweep(c, mu, nx, ny, nz, dx, dy, dz,\n                 [=](const size_t, const double cv, const double lap) {\n                     return 4.5 * ((cv + 1.0) * e_AA + (cv - 1.0) * e_BB - 2.0 * cv * e_AB)\n                            + 3.0 * cv + cv * cv * cv - gamma * lap;\n                 });\n}\n\n// Cahn-Hilliard update step\nstatic inline void cahnHilliardUpdate(double* __restrict cnew, const double* __restrict cold,\n                                      const double* __restrict mu,\n                                      const size_t nx, field (also performs the NUMA first touch of all\n// buffers, using the same distribution as the compute sweeps).\nstatic void initializeConcentration(double* __restrict c, double* __restrict cnew, systems.\ntemplate <typename Body>\nstatic inline void stencilSweep(const double* __restrict in, double* __restrict out,\n                                const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,\n                                const double dx, const double dy, const double dz,\n                                Body body) {\n    const double dx2 = dx * dx;\n    const double dy2 = dy * dy;\n    const double dz2 = dz * dz;\n    const size_t slab = nx * ny;\n\n#pragma omp for collapse(2) schedule(static) nowait\n    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {\n        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {\n            const size_t base = z * slab + y * nx;\n            const size_t zpb = ((z < nz - 1) ? z + 1 : z) * slab + y * nx;\n            const size_t znb = ((z > 0) ? z - 1 : 0) * slab + y * nx;\n            const size_t ypb = z * slab + ((y < ny - 1) ? y + 1 : y) * nx;\n            const size_t ynb = z * slab + ((y > 0) ? y - 1 : 0) * nx;\n\n            const double* __restrict cc = in + base;\n            const double* __restrict cyp = in + ypb;\n            const double* __restrict cyn = in + ynb;\n            const double* __restrict czp = in + zpb;\n            const double* __restrict czn = in + znb;\n            double* __restrict o = out + base;\n\n            // x = 0 (clamped low edge)\n            {\n                const double c0 = cc[0];\n                const double lap = (cc[(nx > 1) ? 1 : 0] + c0 - 2.0 * c0) / dx2\n                                 + (cyp[0] + cyn[0] - 2.0 * c0) / dy2\n                                 + (czp[0] + czn[0] - 2.0 * c0) / dz2;\n                o[0] = body(base, c0, lap);\n            }\n\n            // interior in x: vectorizable, no boundary checks\n            for (size_t x = 1; x + 1 < nx; ++x) {\n                const double c0 = cc[x];\n                const double lap = (cc[x + 1] + cc[x - 1] - 2.0 * c0) / dx2\n                                 + (cyp[x] + cyn[x] - 2.0 * c0) / dy2\n                                 + (czp[x] + czn[x] - 2.0 * c0) / dz2;\n                o[x] = body(base + x, c0, lap);\n            }\n\n            // x = nx - 1 (clamped high edge)\n            if (nx > 1) {\n                const size_t x = nx - 1;\n                const double c0 = cc[x];\n                const double lap = (c0 + cc[x - 1] - 2.0 * c0) / dx2\n                                 + (cyp[x] + cyn[x] - 2.0 * c0) / dy2\n                                 + (czp[x] + czn[x] - 2.0 * c0) / dz2;\n                o[x] = body(base + x, c0, lap);\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Compute chemical potential\nstatic inline void computeChemicalPotential(const double* __restrict c, double* __restrict mu,\n                                            const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,\n                                            const double dx, const double dy, const double dz,\n                                            const double gamma, const double e_AA, const double e_BB, const double e_AB) {\n    stencilSweep(c, mu, nx, ny, nz, dx, dy, dz,\n                 [=](const size_t, const double cv, const double lap) {\n                     return 4.5 * ((cv + 1.0) * e_AA + (cv - 1.0) * e_BB - 2.0 * cv * e_AB)\n                            + 3.0 * cv + cv * cv * cv - gamma * lap;\n                 });\n}\n\n// Cahn-Hilliard update step\nstatic inline void cahnHilliardUpdate(double* __restrict cnew, const double* __restrict cold,\n                                      const double* __restrict mu,\n                                      const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,\n                                      const double D, const double dt, const double dx, const double dy, const double dz) {\n    stencilSweep(mu, cnew, nx, ny, nz, dx, dy, dz,\n                 [=](const size_t idx, const double, const double lap) {\n                     return cold[idx] + dt * D * lap;\n                 });\n}\n\n// Initialize concentration field (also performs the NUMA first touch of all\n// buffers, using the same distribution as the compute sweeps).\nstatic void initializeConcentration(double* __restrict c, double* __restrict cnew, double* __restrict mu,\n                                    const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {\n    const size_t vol = nx * ny * nz;\n    const size_t slab = nx * ny;\n\n#pragma omp parallel for collapse(2) schedule(static) proc_bind(spread)\n    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {\n        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {\n            const size_t base = z * slab + y * nx;\n            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {\n                const size_t idx = base + x;\n                // Generate pseudo-random value in [-1, 1]\n                const size_t linear_id = idx;\n                const double pseudo = ((((linear_id + 1) * 1299709) % vol) / static_cast<double>(vol));\n                c[idx] = -1.0 + 2.0 * pseudo;\n                cnew[idx] = 0.0;\n                mu[idx] = 0.0;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nbool validateResult(const std::vector<double>& c, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nx, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t ny, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nz) {\n    const size_t n = c.size();\n    const double* __restrict d = c.data();\n\n    // Check for NaN or Inf\n    int bad = 0;\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) reduction(|:bad)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        if (std::isnan(d[i]) || std::isinf(d[i])) {\n            bad = 1;\n        }\n    }\n    if (bad) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    // Check if values are in reasonable range for concentration field\n    // After Cahn-Hilliard evolution, values should typically remain bounded\n    double minVal = c[0];\n    double maxVal = c[0];\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) reduction(min:minVal) reduction(max:maxVal)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        minVal = std::min(minVal, d[i]);\n        maxVal = std::max(maxVal, d[i]);\n    }\n\n    printf(\"Concentration range: [%.6f, %.6f]\\n\", minVal, maxVal);\n\n    // Values should generally stay within reasonable bounds\n    if (maxVal > 10.0 || minVal < -10.0) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: values out of expected range\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -x <num>     Grid size in X dimension (default: 64)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -y <num>     Grid size in Y dimension (default: same as X)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -z <num>     Grid size in Z dimension (default: same as X)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -i <num>     Number of time steps (default: 20)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    size_t nx = 64;\n    size_t ny = 0;\n    size_t nz = 0;\n    int iterations = 20;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n\n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-x\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            nx = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-y\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            ny = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-z\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            nz = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-i\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            iterations = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {\n            validate = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {\n            printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n\n    if (ny == 0) ny = nx;\n    if (nz == 0) nz = nx;\n\n    printf(\"Cahn-Hilliard Phase Separation Benchmark\\n\");\n    printf(\"Grid size: %zu x %zu x %zu\\n\", nx, ny, nz);\n    printf(\"Time steps: %d\\n\", iterations);\n    printf(\"OpenMP threads: %d\\n\", omp_get_max_threads());\n    printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? \"enabled\" : \"disabled\");\n\n    // Physical parameters\n    const double dx = 1.0;\n    const double dy = 1.0;\n    const double dz = 1.0;\n    const double dt = 0.01;\n    const double e_AA = -(2.0 / 9.0);\n    const double e_BB = -(2.0 / 9.0);\n    const double e_AB = (2.0 / 9.0);\n    const double gamma = 0.5;\n    const double D = 1.0;\n\n    size_t gridSize = nx * ny * nz;\n\n    // Allocate arrays. Raw (uninitialized) allocation lets the parallel\n    // initialization below place each page on the NUMA node of the thread\n    // that will work on it.\n    const size_t bytes = ((gridSize * sizeof(double)) + 63) & ~static_cast<size_t>(63);\n    double* cold = static_cast<double*>(std::aligned_alloc(64, bytes));\n    double* cnew = static_cast<double*>(std::aligned_alloc(64, bytes));\n    double* mu = static_cast<double*>(std::aligned_alloc(64, bytes));\n    if (!cold || !cnew || !mu) {\n        printf(\"Allocation failed\\n\");\n        return 1;\n    }\n\n    // Initialize concentration field\n    printf(\"Initializing concentration field...\\n\");\n    initializeConcentration(cold, cnew, mu, nx, ny, nz);\n\n    // Run simulation\n    printf(\"Running Cahn-Hilliard simulation...\\n\");\n    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n\n    // One parallel region for the whole time loop: threads are forked once\n    // instead of twice per time step.\n#pragma omp parallel proc_bind(spread)\n    {\n        for (int t = 0; t < iterations; ++t) {\n            // Compute chemical potential\n            computeChemicalPotential(cold, mu, nx, ny, nz, dx, dy, dz,\n                                    gamma, e_AA, e_BB, e_AB);\n#pragma omp barrier\n\n            // Update concentration\n            cahnHilliardUpdate(cnew, cold, mu, nx, ny, nz, D, dt, dx, dy, dz);\n#pragma omp barrier\n\n            // Swap buffers\n#pragma omp single\n            {\n                std::swap(cold, cnew);\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);\n\n    printf(\"Computation time: %ld ms\\n\", duration.count());\n\n    // Calculate performance\n    double cellUpdates = (double)gridSize * iterations;\n    double mcups = cellUpdates / (duration.count() / 1000.0) / 1e6;\n    printf(\"Performance: %.3f MCellUpdates/s\\n\", mcups);\n\n    // Print results for external validation\n    if (printResults || validate) {\n        const std::vector<double> result(cold, cold + gridSize);\n\n        if (printResults) {\n            print_results(result, \"Concentration\");\n        }\n\n        // Validation\n        if (validate) {\n            printf(\"Validating result...\\n\");\n            bool valid = validateResult(result, nx, ny, nz);\n\n            std::free(cold);\n            std::free(cnew);\n            std::free(mu);\n\n            if (valid) {\n                printf(\"Validation: PASSED\\n\");\n                return 0;\n            } else {\n                printf(\"Validation: FAILED\\n\");\n                return 1;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    std::free(cold);\n    std::free(cnew);\n    std::free(mu);\n\n    return 0;\n}\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":286,"oldLines":6,"newStart":286,"newLines":7,"lines":[" ","     // Initialize concentration field","     printf(\"Initializing concentration field...\\n\");","+    bindThreadsToCores();","     initializeConcentration(cold, cnew, mu, nx, ny, nz);"," ","     // Run simulation"]}],"userModified":false,"replaceAll":false}}

The (z, y) plane of rows is distributed with a static\n65\t// schedule, so every sweep (and the initialization) touches the exact same\n66\t// memory on the same thread, which keeps pages local on NUMA systems.\n67\ttemplate <typename Body>\n68\tstatic inline void stencilSweep(const double* __restrict in, double* __restrict out,\n69\t                                const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,\n70\t                                const double dx, const double dy, const double dz,\n71\t                                Body body) {\n72\t    const double dx2 = dx * dx;\n73\t    const double dy2 = dy * dy;\n74\t    const double dz2 = dz * dz;\n75\t    const size_t slab = nx * ny;\n76\t\n77\t#pragma omp for collapse(2) schedule(static) nowait\n78\t    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {\n79\t        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {\n80\t            const size_t base = z * slab + y * nx;\n81\t            const size_t zpb = ((z < nz - 1) ? z + 1 : z) * slab + y * nx;\n82\t            const size_t znb = ((z > 0) ? z - 1 : 0) * slab + y * nx;\n83\t            const size_t ypb = z * slab + ((y < ny - 1) ? y + 1 : y) * nx;\n84\t            const size_t ynb = z * slab + ((y > 0) ? y - 1 : 0) * nx;\n85\t\n86\t            const double* __restrict cc = in + base;\n87\t            const double* __restrict cyp = in + ypb;\n88\t            const double* __restrict cyn = in + ynb;\n89\t            const double* __restrict czp = in + zpb;\n90\t            const double* __restrict czn = in + znb;\n91\t            double* __restrict o = out + base;\n92\t\n93\t            // x = 0 (clamped low edge)\n94\t            {\n95\t                const double c0 = cc[0];\n96\t                const double lap = (cc[(nx > 1) ? 1 : 0] + c0 - 2.0 * c0) / dx2\n97\t                                 + (cyp[0] + cyn[0] - 2.0 * c0) / dy2\n98\t                                 + (czp[0] + czn[0] - 2.0 * c0) / dz2;\n99\t                o[0] = body(base, c0, lap);\n100\t            }\n101\t\n102\t            // interior in x: vectorizable, no boundary checks\n103\t            for (size_t x = 1; x + 1 < nx; ++x) {\n104\t                const double c0 = cc[x];\n105\t                const double lap = (cc[x + 1] + cc[x - 1] - 2.0 * c0) / dx2\n106\t                                 + (cyp[x] + cyn[x] - 2.0 * c0) / dy2\n107\t                                 + (czp[x] + czn[x] - 2.0 * c0) / dz2;\n108\t                o[x] = body(base + x, c0, lap);\n109\t            }\n110\t\n111\t            // x = nx - 1 (clamped high edge)\n112\t            if (nx > 1) {\n113\t                const size_t x = nx - 1;\n114\t                const double c0 = cc[x];\n115\t                const double lap = (c0 + cc[x - 1] - 2.0 * c0) / dx2\n116\t                                 + (cyp[x] + cyn[x] - 2.0 * c0) / dy2\n117\t                                 + (czp[x] + czn[x] - 2.0 * c0) / dz2;\n118\t                o[x] = body(base + x, c0, lap);\n119\t            }\n120\t        }\n121\t    }\n122\t}\n123\t\n124\t// Compute chemical potential\n125\tstatic inline void computeChemicalPotential(const double* __restrict c, double* __restrict mu,\n126\t                                            const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,\n127\t                                            const double dx, const double dy, const double dz,\n128\t                                            const double gamma, const double e_AA, const double e_BB, const double e_AB) {\n129\t    stencilSweep(c, mu, nx, ny, nz, dx, dy, dz,\n130\t                 [=](const size_t, const double cv, const double lap) {\n131\t                     return 4.5 * ((cv + 1.0) * e_AA + (cv - 1.0) * e_BB - 2.0 * cv * e_AB)\n132\t                            + 3.0 * cv + cv * cv * cv - gamma * lap;\n133\t                 });\n134\t}\n135\t\n136\t// Cahn-Hilliard update step\n137\tstatic inline void cahnHilliardUpdate(double* __restrict cnew, const double* __restrict cold,\n138\t i < CPU_SETSIZE; ++i) {\n        if (CPU_ISSET(i, &allowed)) cpus.push_back(i);\n    }\n    if (cpus.empty()) return;\n    const int ncpus = static_cast<int>(cpus.size());\n\n#pragma omp parallel\n    {\n        const int tid = omp_get_thread_num();\n        const int nthreads = omp_get_num_threads();\n        // Spread: with fewer threads than CPUs, stride over the CPU list so\n        // both sockets are used before any SMT sibling pair is doubled up.\n        const int slot = (nthreads <= ncpus)\n                             ? static_cast<int>(static_cast<long long>(tid) * ncpus / nthreads)\n                             : (tid % ncpus);\n        cpu_set_t set;\n        CPU_ZERO(&set);\n        CPU_SET(cpus[slot], &set);\n        sched_setaffinity(0, sizeof(set), &set);\n    }\n#endif\n}\n\n// 3D index calculation\ninline constexpr size_t idx3(const size_t x, const size_t y, const size_t z, const size_t nx, const size_t ny) noexcept {\n    return z * (nx * ny) + y * nx + x;\n}\n\n// One row (fixed y, z) of the 7-point Laplacian stencil with clamped boundary\n// conditions in x. `cc` is the row itself, the other four pointers are the\n// already clamped neighbour rows in y and z. `body(index, value, laplacian)`\n// produces the output value; `base` is the linear index of the row start.\ntemplate <typename Body>\nstatic inline void stencilRow(const double* __restrict cc, const double* __restrict cyp,\n                              const double* __restrict cyn, const double* __restrict czp,\n                              const double* __restrict czn, double* __restrict o,\n                              const size_t nx, const size_t base,\n                              const double dx2, const double dy2, const double dz2,\n                              Body body) {\n    // x = 0 (clamped low edge)\n    {\n        const double c0 = cc[0];\n        const double lap = (cc[(nx > 1) ? 1 : 0] + c0 - 2.0 * c0) / dx2\n                         + (cyp[0] + cyn[0] - 2.0 * c0) / dy2\n                         + (czp[0] + czn[0] - 2.0 * c0) / dz2;\n        o[0] = body(base, c0, lap);\n    }\n\n    // interior in x: vectorizable, no boundary checks\n    for (size_t x = 1; x + 1 < nx; ++x) {\n        const double c0 = cc[x];\n        const double lap = (cc[x + 1] + cc[x - 1] - 2.0 * c0) / dx2\n                         + (cyp[x] + cyn[x] - 2.0 * c0) / dy2\n                         + (czp[x] + czn[x] - 2.0 * c0) / dz2;\n        o[x] = body(base + x, c0, lap);\n    }\n\n    // x = nx - 1 (clamped high edge)\n    if (nx > 1) {\n        const size_t x = nx - 1;\n        const double c0 = cc[x];\n        const double lap = (c0 + cc[x - 1] - 2.0 * c0) / dx2\n                         + (cyp[x] + cyn[x] - 2.0 * c0) / dy2\n                         + (czp[x] + czn[x] - 2.0 * c0) / dz2;\n        o[x] = body(base + x, c0, lap);\n    }\n}\n\n// Decomposition of the (y, z) plane of grid rows into tiles, one tile per\n// thread (or a few more). Tiles are kept roughly square so that the halo the\n// fused kernel has to recompute stays small.\nstruct Tiling {\n    size_t by = 1, bz = 1;    // tile extent in y and z\n    size_t nby = 1, nbz = 1;  // number of tiles per direction\n    size_t ntiles = 1;\n    bool fused = false;       // worth running the fused (cache blocked) kernel?\n};\n\nstatic Tiling makeTiling(const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz, const int nthreads) {\n    Tiling t;\n\n    // The fused kernel keeps three planes of `by + 2` rows of mu live; size the\n    // tile so that ring buffer stays comfortably inside a private L2 cache.\n    const size_t kRingBudget = 384 * 1024;  // bytes\n    size_t byMax = kRingBudget / (3 * sizeof(double) * std::max<size_t>(nx, 1));\n    byMax = std::max<size_t>(byMax, 4) - 2;\n    byMax = std::max<size_t>(byMax, 1);\n\n    // Aim for `nthreads` roughly square tiles.\n    const double area = static_cast<double>(ny) * static_cast<double>(nz) / std::max(nthreads, 1);\n    size_t by = static_cast<size_t>(std::sqrt(area * static_cast<double>(ny) / static_cast<double>(nz)));\n    by = std::clamp<size_t>(by, 1, std::min(ny, byMax));\n    t.nby = (ny + by - 1) / by;\n    t.by = (ny + t.nby - 1) / t.nby;\n\n    size_t bz = static_cast<size_t>(std::ceil(static_cast<double>(nz) * t.nby / std::max(nthreads, 1)));\n    bz = std::clamp<size_t>(bz, 1, nz);\n    t.nbz = (nz + bz - 1) / bz;\n    t.bz = (nz + t.nbz - 1) / t.nbz;\n\n    t.ntiles = t.nby * t.nbz;\n\n    // Fusing trades memory traffic for recomputation of the tile halo. It only\n    // pays off once the tiles are big enough for that halo to be a small\n    // fraction of the tile, and once the grid no longer fits in cache anyway.\n    const double halo = static_cast<double>(t.by + 4) * (t.bz + 4) / (static_cast<double>(t.by) * t.bz);\n    t.fused = (t.by >= 8 && t.bz >= 8 && halo <= 1.7);\n    return t;\n}\n\n// Bounds of tile number `tile` in the (y, z) plane.\nstatic inline void tileBounds(const Tiling& t, const size_t tile, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,\n                              size_t& y0, size_t& y1, size_t& z0, size_t& z1) {\n    const size_t ty = tile % t.nby;\n    const size_t tz = tile / t.nby;\n    y0 = std::min(ty * t.by, ny);\n    y1 = std::min(y0 + t.by, ny);\n    z0 = std::min(tz * t.bz, nz);\n    z1 = std::min(z0 + t.bz, nz);\n}\n\n// Non-fused reference path: one full sweep of the grid, distributed over the\n// same tiles as everything else so that the NUMA placement stays consistent.\n//\n// This is an orphaned worksharing construct: call it from a parallel region.\ntemplate <typename Body>\nstatic inline void stencilSweep(const double* __restrict in, double* __restrict out,\n                                const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,\n                                const double dx2, const double dy2, const double dz2,\n                                const Tiling& t, Body body) {\n    const size_t slab = nx * ny;\n\n#pragma omp for schedule(static) nowait\n    for (size_t tile = 0; tile < t.ntiles; ++tile) {\n        size_t y0, y1, z0, z1;\n        tileBounds(t, tile, ny, nz, y0, y1, z0, z1);\n\n        for (size_t z = z0; z < z1; ++z) {\n            const size_t zp = ((z < nz - 1) ? z + 1 : z) * slab;\n            const size_t zn = ((z > 0) ? z - 1 : 0) * slab;\n            for (size_t y = y0; y < y1; ++y) {\n                const size_t base = z * slab + y * nx;\n                const size_t yp = ((y < ny - 1) ? y + 1 : y) * nx;\n                const size_t yn = ((y > 0) ? y - 1 : 0) * nx;\n                stencilRow(in + base, in + z * slab + yp, in + z * slab + yn,\n                           in + zp + y * nx, in + zn + y * nx, out + base,\n                           nx, base, dx2, dy2, dz2, body);\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Fused time step: for every tile, mu is produced plane by plane into a small\n// per-thread ring buffer that stays in cache, and the concentration update\n// consumes it immediately. Compared with two full sweeps this removes all\n// memory traffic for mu (write + read) and the second read of the\n// concentration field, at the cost of recomputing mu on a one row wide halo\n// around each tile. The arithmetic per cell is unchanged, so results are\n// bit-identical to the non-fused path.\n//\n// This is an orphaned worksharing construct: call it from a parallel region.\nstatic inline void fusedStep(const double* __restrict cold, double* __restrict cnew,\n                             double* __restrict ring, const size_t ringRows,\n                             const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,\n                             const double dx2, const double dy2, const double dz2,\n                             const double gamma, const double e_AA, const double e_BB, const double e_AB,\n                             const double D, const double dt, const Tiling& t) {\n    const size_t slab = nx * ny;\n    const size_t ringPlane = ringRows * nx;\n\n#pragma omp for schedule(static) nowait\n    for (size_t tile = 0; tile < t.ntiles; ++tile) {\n        size_t y0, y1, z0, z1;\n        tileBounds(t, tile, ny, nz, y0, y1, z0, z1);\n        if (y0 >= y1 || z0 >= z1) continue;\n\n        // rows of mu this tile needs (its own rows plus one halo row per side)\n        const size_t ylo = (y0 > 0) ? y0 - 1 : 0;\n        const size_t yhi = (y1 < ny) ? y1 + 1 : ny;\n\n        const size_t pstart = (z0 > 0) ? z0 - 1 : 0;\n        const size_t pend = (z1 < nz) ? z1 : nz - 1;  // inclusive\n\n        for (size_t p = pstart; p <= pend; ++p) {\n            // --- produce mu for plane p into ring slot p % 3 ---\n            double* __restrict dst = ring + (p % 3) * ringPlane;\n            const size_t zp = ((p < nz - 1) ? p + 1 : p) * slab;\n            const size_t zn = ((p > 0) ? p - 1 : 0) * slab;\n            for (size_t y = ylo; y < yhi; ++y) {\n                const size_t base = p * slab + y * nx;\n                const size_t yp = ((y < ny - 1) ? y + 1 : y) * nx;\n                const size_t yn = ((y > 0) ? y - 1 : 0) * nx;\n                stencilRow(cold + base, cold + p * slab + yp, cold + p * slab + yn,\n                           cold + zp + y * nx, cold + zn + y * nx,\n                           dst + (y - ylo) * nx, nx, 0, dx2, dy2, dz2,\n                           [=](const size_t, const double cv, const double lap) {\n                               return 4.5 * ((cv + 1.0) * e_AA + (cv - 1.0) * e_BB - 2.0 * cv * e_AB)\n                                      + 3.0 * cv + cv * cv * cv - gamma * lap;\n                           });\n            }\n\n            // --- consume: every plane whose mu neighbourhood is complete ---\n            // plane p - 1 becomes available once plane p is done; the last\n            // plane of the grid clamps upwards and is available immediately.\n            for (int pass = 0; pass < 2; ++pass) {\n                size_t z;\n                if (pass == 0) {\n                    if (p < z0 + 1) continue;\n                    z = p - 1;\n                } else {\n                    if (p != nz - 1 || z1 != nz) continue;\n                    z = nz - 1;\n                }\n\n                const double* __restrict mzp = ring + (((z < nz - 1) ? z + 1 : z) % 3) * ringPlane;\n                const double* __restrict mzn = ring + (((z > 0) ? z - 1 : 0) % 3) * ringPlane;\n                const double* __restrict mcc = ring + (z % 3) * ringPlane;\n                for (size_t y = y0; y < y1; ++y) {\n                    const size_t base = z * slab + y * nx;\n                    const size_t r = (y - ylo) * nx;\n                    const size_t rp = (((y < ny - 1) ? y + 1 : y) - ylo) * nx;\n                    const size_t rn = (((y > 0) ? y - 1 : 0) - ylo) * nx;\n                    stencilRow(mcc + r, mcc + rp, mcc + rn, mzp + r, mzn + r,\n                               cnew + base, nx, base, dx2, dy2, dz2,\n                               [=](const size_t idx, const double, const double lap) {\n                                   return cold[idx] + dt * D * lap;\n                               });\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Initialize concentration field (also performs the NUMA first touch of all\n// buffers, using the same tile distribution as the compute kernels).\nstatic void initializeConcentration(double* __restrict c, double* __restrict cnew, double* __restrict mu,\n                                    const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz, const Tiling& t) {\n    const size_t vol = nx * ny * nz;\n    const size_t slab = nx * ny;\n\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) proc_bind(spread)\n    for (size_t tile = 0; tile < t.ntiles; ++tile) {\n        size_t y0, y1, z0, z1;\n        tileBounds(t, tile, ny, nz, y0, y1, z0, z1);\n\n        for (size_t z = z0; z < z1; ++z) {\n            for (size_t y = y0; y < y1; ++y) {\n                const size_t base = z * slab + y * nx;\n                for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {\n                    const size_t idx = base + x;\n                    // Generate pseudo-random value in [-1, 1]\n                    const size_t linear_id = idx;\n                    const double pseudo = ((((linear_id + 1) * 1299709) % vol) / static_cast<double>(vol));\n                    c[idx] = -1.0 + 2.0 * pseudo;\n                    cnew[idx] = 0.0;\n                    if (mu) mu[idx] = 0.0;\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nbool validateResult(const std::vector<double>& c, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nx, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t ny, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nz) {\n    const size_t n = c.size();\n    const double* __restrict d = c.data();\n\n    // Check for NaN or Inf\n    int bad = 0;\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) reduction(|:bad)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        if (std::isnan(d[i]) || std::isinf(d[i])) {\n            bad = 1;\n        }\n    }\n    if (bad) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    // Check if values are in reasonable range for concentration field\n    // After Cahn-Hilliard evolution, values should typically remain bounded\n    double minVal = c[0];\n    double maxVal = c[0];\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) reduction(min:minVal) reduction(max:maxVal)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        minVal = std::min(minVal, d[i]);\n        maxVal = std::max(maxVal, d[i]);\n    }\n\n    printf(\"Concentration range: [%.6f, %.6f]\\n\", minVal, maxVal);\n\n    // Values should generally stay within reasonable bounds\n    if (maxVal > 10.0 || minVal < -10.0) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: values out of expected range\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -x <num>     Grid size in X dimension (default: 64)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -y <num>     Grid size in Y dimension (default: same as X)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -z <num>     Grid size in Z dimension (default: same as X)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -i <num>     Number of time steps (default: 20)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    size_t nx = 64;\n    size_t ny = 0;\n    size_t nz = 0;\n    int iterations = 20;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n\n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1;                             const double gamma, const double e_AA, const double e_BB, const double e_AB,\n                                            const Tiling& t) {\n    stencilSweep(c, mu, nx, ny, nz, dx2, dy2, dz2, t,\n                 [=](const size_t, const double cv, const double lap) {\n                     return 4.5 * ((cv + 1.0) * e_AA + (cv - 1.0) * e_BB - 2.0 * cv * e_AB)\n                            + 3.0 * cv + cv * cv * cv - gamma * lap;\n                 });\n}\n\n// Cahn-Hilliard update step (non-fused path)\nstatic inline void cahnHilliardUpdate(double* __restrict cnew, const double* __restrict cold,\n                                      const double* __restrict mu,\n                                      const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,\n                                      const double D, const double dt,\n                                      const double dx2, const double dy2, const double dz2,\n                                      const Tiling& t) {\n    stencilSweep(mu, cnew, nx, ny, nz, dx2, dy2, dz2, t,\n                 [=](const size_t idx, const double, const double lap) {\n                     return cold[idx] + dt * D * lap;\n                 });\n}\n\n// Fused time step: for every tile,","originalFile":"#include <algorithm>\n#include <chrono>\n#include <cmath>\n#include <cstdio>\n#include <cstdlib>\n#include <cstring>\n#include <vector>\n\n#include <omp.h>\n\n#ifdef __linux__\n#include <sched.h>\n#endif\n\n#include \"../common/results_output.hpp\"\n\n// Pin every OpenMP thread to a distinct CPU, spread over the available cores.\n// This kernel is memory bound, so keeping threads (and thus the pages they\n// first touch) on a fixed NUMA node is worth several times the performance.\n// Only done when the user did not request an affinity policy: libgomp's\n// default is OMP_PROC_BIND=false with an empty place list, in which case the\n// proc_bind clauses below have no effect and threads are free to migrate.\nstatic void bindThreadsToCores() {\n#ifdef __linux__\n    if (omp_get_proc_bind() != omp_proc_bind_false) return;\n\n    cpu_set_t allowed;\n    CPU_ZERO(&allowed);\n    if (sched_getaffinity(0, sizeof(allowed), &allowed) != 0) return;\n\n    std::vector<int> cpus;\n    for (int i = 0; i < CPU_SETSIZE; ++i) {\n        if (CPU_ISSET(i, &allowed)) cpus.push_back(i);\n    }\n    if (cpus.empty()) return;\n    const int ncpus = static_cast<int>(cpus.size());\n\n#pragma omp parallel\n    {\n        const int tid = omp_get_thread_num();\n        const int nthreads = omp_get_num_threads();\n        // Spread: with fewer threads than CPUs, stride over the CPU list so\n        // both sockets are used before any SMT sibling pair is doubled up.\n        const int slot = (nthreads <= ncpus)\n                             ? static_cast<int>(static_cast<long long>(tid) * ncpus / nthreads)\n                             : (tid % ncpus);\n        cpu_set_t set;\n        CPU_ZERO(&set);\n        CPU_SET(cpus[slot], &set);\n        sched_setaffinity(0, sizeof(set), &set);\n    }\n#endif\n}\n\n// 3D index calculation\ninline constexpr size_t idx3(const size_t x, const size_t y, const size_t z, const size_t nx, const size_t ny) noexcept {\n    return z * (nx * ny) + y * nx + x;\n}\n\n// One row (fixed y, z) of the 7-point Laplacian stencil with clamped boundary\n// conditions in x. `cc` is the row itself, the other four pointers are the\n// already clamped neighbour rows in y and z. `body(index, value, laplacian)`\n// produces the output value; `base` is the linear index of the row start.\ntemplate <typename Body>\nstatic inline void stencilRow(const double* __restrict cc, const double* __restrict cyp,\n                              const double* __restrict cyn, const double* __restrict czp,\n                              const double* __restrict czn, double* __restrict o,\n                              const size_t nx, const size_t base,\n                              const double dx2, const double dy2, const double dz2,\n                              Body body) {\n    // x = 0 (clamped low edge)\n    {\n        const double c0 = cc[0];\n        const double lap = (cc[(nx > 1) ? 1 : 0] + c0 - 2.0 * c0) / dx2\n                         + (cyp[0] + cyn[0] - 2.0 * c0) / dy2\n                         + (czp[0] + czn[0] - 2.0 * c0) / dz2;\n        o[0] = body(base, c0, lap);\n    }\n\n    // interior in x: vectorizable, no boundary checks\n    for (size_t x = 1; x + 1 < nx; ++x) {\n        const double c0 = cc[x];\n        const double lap = (cc[x + 1] + cc[x - 1] - 2.0 * c0) / dx2\n                         + (cyp[x] + cyn[x] - 2.0 * c0) / dy2\n                         + (czp[x] + czn[x] - 2.0 * c0) / dz2;\n        o[x] = body(base + x, c0, lap);\n    }\n\n    // x = nx - 1 (clamped high edge)\n    if (nx > 1) {\n        const size_t x = nx - 1;\n        const double c0 = cc[x];\n        const double lap = (c0 + cc[x - 1] - 2.0 * c0) / dx2\n                         + (cyp[x] + cyn[x] - 2.0 * c0) / dy2\n                         + (czp[x] + czn[x] - 2.0 * c0) / dz2;\n        o[x] = body(base + x, c0, lap);\n    }\n}\n\n// Decomposition of the (y, z) plane of grid rows into tiles, one tile per\n// thread (or a few more). Tiles are kept roughly square so that the halo the\n// fused kernel has to recompute stays small.\nstruct Tiling {\n    size_t by = 1, bz = 1;    // tile extent in y and z\n    size_t nby = 1, nbz = 1;  // number of tiles per direction\n    size_t ntiles = 1;\n    bool fused = false;       // worth running the fused (cache blocked) kernel?\n};\n\nstatic Tiling makeTiling(const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz, const int nthreads) {\n    Tiling t;\n\n    // The fused kernel keeps three planes of `by + 2` rows of mu live; size the\n    // tile so that ring buffer stays comfortably inside a private L2 cache.\n    const size_t kRingBudget = 384 * 1024;  // bytes\n    size_t byMax = kRingBudget / (3 * sizeof(double) * std::max<size_t>(nx, 1));\n    byMax = std::max<size_t>(byMax, 4) - 2;\n    byMax = std::max<size_t>(byMax, 1);\n\n    // Aim for `nthreads` roughly square tiles.\n    const double area = static_cast<double>(ny) * static_cast<double>(nz) / std::max(nthreads, 1);\n    size_t by = static_cast<size_t>(std::sqrt(area * static_cast<double>(ny) / static_cast<double>(nz)));\n    by = std::clamp<size_t>(by, 1, std::min(ny, byMax));\n    t.nby = (ny + by - 1) / by;\n    t.by = (ny + t.nby - 1) / t.nby;\n\n    size_t bz = static_cast<size_t>(std::ceil(static_cast<double>(nz) * t.nby / std::max(nthreads, 1)));\n    bz = std::clamp<size_t>(bz, 1, nz);\n    t.nbz = (nz + bz - 1) / bz;\n    t.bz = (nz + t.nbz - 1) / t.nbz;\n\n    t.ntiles = t.nby * t.nbz;\n\n    // Fusing trades memory traffic for recomputation of the tile halo. It only\n    // pays off once the tiles are big enough for that halo to be a small\n    // fraction of the tile, and once the grid no longer fits in cache anyway.\n    const double halo = static_cast<double>(t.by + 4) * (t.bz + 4) / (static_cast<double>(t.by) * t.bz);\n    t.fused = (t.by >= 8 && t.bz >= 8 && halo <= 1.7);\n    return t;\n}\n\n// Bounds of tile number `tile` in the (y, z) plane.\nstatic inline void tileBounds(const Tiling& t, const size_t tile, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,\n                              size_t& y0, size_t& y1, size_t& z0, size_t& z1) {\n    const size_t ty = tile % t.nby;\n    const size_t tz = tile / t.nby;\n    y0 = std::min(ty * t.by, ny);\n    y1 = std::min(y0 + t.by, ny);\n    z0 = std::min(tz * t.bz, nz);\n    z1 = std::min(z0 + t.bz, nz);\n}\n\n// Non-fused reference path: one full sweep of the grid, distributed over the\n// same tiles as everything else so that the NUMA placement stays consistent.\n//\n// This is an orphaned worksharing construct: call it from a parallel region.\ntemplate <typename Body>\nstatic inline void stencilSweep(const double* __restrict in, double* __restrict out,\n                                const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,\n                                const double dx2, const double dy2, const double dz2,\n                                const Tiling& t, Body body) {\n    const size_t slab = nx * ny;\n\n#pragma omp for schedule(static) nowait\n    for (size_t tile = 0; tile < t.ntiles; ++tile) {\n        size_t y0, y1, z0, z1;\n        tileBounds(t, tile, ny, nz, y0, y1, z0, z1);\n\n        for (size_t z = z0; z < z1; ++z) {\n            const size_t zp = ((z < nz - 1) ? z + 1 : z) * slab;\n            const size_t zn = ((z > 0) ? z - 1 : 0) * slab;\n            for (size_t y = y0; y < y1; ++y) {\n                const size_t base = z * slab + y * nx;\n                const size_t yp = ((y < ny - 1) ? y + 1 : y) * nx;\n                const size_t yn = ((y > 0) ? y - 1 : 0) * nx;\n                stencilRow(in + base, in + z * slab + yp, in + z * slab + yn,\n                           in + zp + y * nx, in + zn + y * nx, out + base,\n                           nx, base, dx2, dy2, dz2, body);\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Fused time step: for every tile, mu is produced plane by plane into a small\n// per-thread ring buffer that stays in cache, and the concentration update\n// consumes it immediately. Compared with two full sweeps this removes all\n// memory traffic for mu (write + read) and the second read of the\n// concentration field, at the cost of recomputing mu on a one row wide halo\n// around each tile. The arithmetic per cell is unchanged, so results are\n// bit-identical to the non-fused path.\n//\n// This is an orphaned worksharing construct: call it from a parallel region.\nstatic inline void fusedStep(const double* __restrict cold, double* __restrict cnew,\n                             double* __restrict ring, const size_t ringRows,\n                             const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,\n                             const double dx2, const double dy2, const double dz2,\n                             const double gamma, const double e_AA, const double e_BB, const double e_AB,\n                             const double D, const double dt, const Tiling& t) {\n    const size_t slab = nx * ny;\n    const size_t ringPlane = ringRows * nx;\n\n#pragma omp for schedule(static) nowait\n    for (size_t tile = 0; tile < t.ntiles; ++tile) {\n        size_t y0, y1, z0, z1;\n        tileBounds(t, tile, ny, nz, y0, y1, z0, z1);\n        if (y0 >= y1 || z0 >= z1) continue;\n\n        // rows of mu this tile needs (its own rows plus one halo row per side)\n        const size_t ylo = (y0 > 0) ? y0 - 1 : 0;\n        const size_t yhi = (y1 < ny) ? y1 + 1 : ny;\n\n        const size_t pstart = (z0 > 0) ? z0 - 1 : 0;\n        const size_t pend = (z1 < nz) ? z1 : nz - 1;  // inclusive\n\n        for (size_t p = pstart; p <= pend; ++p) {\n            // --- produce mu for plane p into ring slot p % 3 ---\n            double* __restrict dst = ring + (p % 3) * ringPlane;\n            const size_t zp = ((p < nz - 1) ? p + 1 : p) * slab;\n            const size_t zn = ((p > 0) ? p - 1 : 0) * slab;\n            for (size_t y = ylo; y < yhi; ++y) {\n                const size_t base = p * slab + y * nx;\n                const size_t yp = ((y < ny - 1) ? y + 1 : y) * nx;\n                const size_t yn = ((y > 0) ? y - 1 : 0) * nx;\n                stencilRow(cold + base, cold + p * slab + yp, cold + p * slab + yn,\n                           cold + zp + y * nx, cold + zn + y * nx,\n                           dst + (y - ylo) * nx, nx, 0, dx2, dy2, dz2,\n                           [=](const size_t, const double cv, const double lap) {\n                               return 4.5 * ((cv + 1.0) * e_AA + (cv - 1.0) * e_BB - 2.0 * cv * e_AB)\n                                      + 3.0 * cv + cv * cv * cv - gamma * lap;\n                           });\n            }\n\n            // --- consume: every plane whose mu neighbourhood is complete ---\n            // plane p - 1 becomes available once plane p is done; the last\n            // plane of the grid clamps upwards and is available immediately.\n            for (int pass = 0; pass < 2; ++pass) {\n                size_t z;\n                if (pass == 0) {\n                    if (p < z0 + 1) continue;\n                    z = p - 1;\n                } else {\n                    if (p != nz - 1 || z1 != nz) continue;\n                    z = nz - 1;\n                }\n\n                const double* __restrict mzp = ring + (((z < nz - 1) ? z + 1 : z) % 3) * ringPlane;\n                const double* __restrict mzn = ring + (((z > 0) ? z - 1 : 0) % 3) * ringPlane;\n                const double* __restrict mcc = ring + (z % 3) * ringPlane;\n                for (size_t y = y0; y < y1; ++y) {\n                    const size_t base = z * slab + y * nx;\n                    const size_t r = (y - ylo) * nx;\n                    const size_t rp = (((y < ny - 1) ? y + 1 : y) - ylo) * nx;\n                    const size_t rn = (((y > 0) ? y - 1 : 0) - ylo) * nx;\n                    stencilRow(mcc + r, mcc + rp, mcc + rn, mzp + r, mzn + r,\n                               cnew + base, nx, base, dx2, dy2, dz2,\n                               [=](const size_t idx, const double, const double lap) {\n                                   return cold[idx] + dt * D * lap;\n                               });\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Initialize concentration field (also performs the NUMA first touch of all\n// buffers, using the same tile distribution as the compute kernels).\nstatic void initializeConcentration(double* __restrict c, double* __restrict cnew, double* __restrict mu,\n                                    const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz, const Tiling& t) {\n    const size_t vol = nx * ny * nz;\n    const size_t slab = nx * ny;\n\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) proc_bind(spread)\n    for (size_t tile = 0; tile < t.ntiles; ++tile) {\n        size_t y0, y1, z0, z1;\n        tileBounds(t, tile, ny, nz, y0, y1, z0, z1);\n\n        for (size_t z = z0; z < z1; ++z) {\n            for (size_t y = y0; y < y1; ++y) {\n                const size_t base = z * slab + y * nx;\n                for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {\n                    const size_t idx = base + x;\n                    // Generate pseudo-random value in [-1, 1]\n                    const size_t linear_id = idx;\n                    const double pseudo = ((((linear_id + 1) * 1299709) % vol) / static_cast<double>(vol));\n                    c[idx] = -1.0 + 2.0 * pseudo;\n                    cnew[idx] = 0.0;\n                    if (mu) mu[idx] = 0.0;\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nbool validateResult(const std::vector<double>& c, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nx, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t ny, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nz) {\n    const size_t n = c.size();\n    const double* __restrict d = c.data();\n\n    // Check for NaN or Inf\n    int bad = 0;\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) reduction(|:bad)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        if (std::isnan(d[i]) || std::isinf(d[i])) {\n            bad = 1;\n        }\n    }\n    if (bad) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    // Check if values are in reasonable range for concentration field\n    // After Cahn-Hilliard evolution, values should typically remain bounded\n    double minVal = c[0];\n    double maxVal = c[0];\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) reduction(min:minVal) reduction(max:maxVal)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        minVal = std::min(minVal, d[i]);\n        maxVal = std::max(maxVal, d[i]);\n    }\n\n    printf(\"Concentration range: [%.6f, %.6f]\\n\", minVal, maxVal);\n\n    // Values should generally stay within reasonable bounds\n    if (maxVal > 10.0 || minVal < -10.0) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: values out of expected range\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -x <num>     Grid size in X dimension (default: 64)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -y <num>     Grid size in Y dimension (default: same as X)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -z <num>     Grid size in Z dimension (default: same as X)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -i <num>     Number of time steps (default: 20)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    size_t nx = 64;\n    size_t ny = 0;\n    size_t nz = 0;\n    int iterations = 20;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n\n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-x\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            nx = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-y\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            ny = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-z\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            nz = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-i\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            iterations = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {\n            validate = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {\n            printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n\n    if (ny == 0) ny = nx;\n    if (nz == 0) nz = nx;\n\n    printf(\"Cahn-Hilliard Phase Separation Benchmark\\n\");\n    printf(\"Grid size: %zu x %zu x %zu\\n\", nx, ny, nz);\n    printf(\"Time steps: %d\\n\", iterations);\n    printf(\"OpenMP threads: %d\\n\", omp_get_max_threads());\n    printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? \"enabled\" : \"disabled\");\n\n    // Physical parameters\n    const double dx = 1.0;\n    const double dy = 1.0;\n    const double dz = 1.0;\n    const double dt = 0.01;\n    const double e_AA = -(2.0 / 9.0);\n    const double e_BB = -(2.0 / 9.0);\n    const double e_AB = (2.0 / 9.0);\n    const double gamma = 0.5;\n    const double D = 1.0;\n\n    size_t gridSize = nx * ny * nz;\n\n    const double dx2 = dx * dx;\n    const double dy2 = dy * dy;\n    const double dz2 = dz * dz;\n\n    bindThreadsToCores();\n    const Tiling tiling = makeTiling(nx, ny, nz, omp_get_max_threads());\n    printf(\"Decomposition: %zu x %zu tiles of %zu x %zu rows (%s)\\n\",\n           tiling.nbz, tiling.nby, tiling.bz, tiling.by, tiling.fused ? \"fused\" : \"two-pass\");\n\n    // Allocate arrays. Raw (uninitialized) allocation lets the parallel\n    // initialization below place each page on the NUMA node of the thread\n    // that will work on it.\n    const size_t bytes = ((gridSize * sizeof(double)) + 63) & ~static_cast<size_t>(63);\n    double* cold = static_cast<double*>(std::aligned_alloc(64, bytes));\n    double* cnew = static_cast<double*>(std::aligned_alloc(64, bytes));\n    // The fused kernel keeps mu in per-thread ring buffers instead.\n    double* mu = tiling.fused ? nullptr : static_cast<double*>(std::aligned_alloc(64, bytes));\n    if (!cold || !cnew || (!tiling.fused && !mu)) {\n        printf(\"Allocation failed\\n\");\n        return 1;\n    }\n\n    // Initialize concentration field\n    printf(\"Initializing concentration field...\\n\");\n    initializeConcentration(cold, cnew, mu, nx, ny, nz, tiling);\n\n    // Run simulation\n    printf(\"Running Cahn-Hilliard simulation...\\n\");\n    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n\n    // Rows of mu a tile needs: its own plus one halo row on each side.\n    const size_t ringRows = std::min(ny, tiling.by + 2);\n\n    // One parallel region for the whole time loop: threads are forked once\n    // instead of twice per time step.\n#pragma omp parallel proc_bind(spread)\n    {\n        double* ring = nullptr;\n        if (tiling.fused) {\n            ring = static_cast<double*>(std::aligned_alloc(64, 3 * ringRows * nx * sizeof(double)));\n        }\n\n        for (int t = 0; t < iterations; ++t) {\n            if (tiling.fused) {\n                fusedStep(cold, cnew, ring, ringRows, nx, ny, nz, dx2, dy2, dz2,\n                          gamma, e_AA, e_BB, e_AB, D, dt, tiling);\n#pragma omp barrier\n            } else {\n                // Compute chemical potential\n                computeChemicalPotential(cold, mu, nx, ny, nz, dx2, dy2, dz2,\n                                         gamma, e_AA, e_BB, e_AB, tiling);\n#pragma omp barrier\n\n                // Update concentration\n                cahnHilliardUpdate(cnew, cold, mu, nx, ny, nz, D, dt, dx2, dy2, dz2, tiling);\n#pragma omp barrier\n            }\n\n            // Swap buffers\n#pragma omp single\n            {\n                std::swap(cold, cnew);\n            }\n        }\n\n        std::free(ring);\n    }\n\n    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);\n\n    printf(\"Computation time: %ld ms\\n\", duration.count());\n\n    // Calculate performance\n    double cellUpdates = (double)gridSize * iterations;\n    double mcups = cellUpdates / (duration.count() / 1000.0) / 1e6;\n    printf(\"Performance: %.3f MCellUpdates/s\\n\", mcups);\n\n    // Print results for external validation\n    if (printResults || validate) {\n        const std::vector<double> result(cold, cold + gridSize);\n\n        if (printResults) {\n            print_results(result, \"Concentration\");\n        }\n\n        // Validation\n        if (validate) {\n            printf(\"Validating result...\\n\");\n            bool valid = validateResult(result, nx, ny, nz);\n\n            std::free(cold);\n            std::free(cnew);\n            std::free(mu);\n\n            if (valid) {\n                printf(\"Validation: PASSED\\n\");\n                return 0;\n            } else {\n                printf(\"Validation: FAILED\\n\");\n                return 1;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    std::free(cold);\n    std::free(cnew);\n    std::free(mu);\n\n    return 0;\n}\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":181,"oldLines":6,"newStart":181,"newLines":32,"lines":["     }"," }"," The halo test guards against grid shapes that would make the\n    // tiles too thin for the recomputation to be affordable.\n    const double workingSet = 3.0 * sizeof(double) * static_cast<double>(nx) * ny * nz;\n    t.fused = (bestScore <= 1.7) && (workingSet > 1.75 * static_cast<double>(totalLastLevelCache()));\n    return t;\n}\n\n// Bounds of tile number `tile` in the (y, z) plane. The split is balanced:\n// tile extents differ by at most one row and no tile is empty.\nstatic inline void tileBounds(const Tiling& t, const size_t tile, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,\n                              size_t& y0, size_t& y1, size_t& z0, size_t& z1) {\n    const size_t ty = tile % t.nby;\n    const size_t tz = tile / t.nby;\n    y0 = ty * ny / t.nby;\n    y1 = (ty + 1) * ny / t.nby;\n    z0 = tz * nz / t.nbz;\n    z1 = (tz + 1) * nz / t.nbz;\n}\n\n'''\nopen(p,'w').write(s[:old_start] + new + s[old_end:])\nPYEOF\ncmake --build build -j 2>&1|tail -3 && for g in 64 128 256 320 384 512 768 1024; do printf \"N=%4d \" $g; ./build/cahn_hilliard -x $g -i 1|grep -oP 'Decomposition: \\K.*'; done","uuid":"8d00d177-7c7f-45e6-a385-d1cb16cc6935","session_id":"b3e9c071-3ee1-4042-b901-3e972721f321"}

{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_014TVLsYF76EzYsGHDad7QuR","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t#include <algorithm>\n2\t#include <chrono>\n3\t#include <cmath>\n4\t#include <cstdio>\n5\t#include <cstdlib>\n6\t#include <cstring>\n7\t#include <vector>\n8\t\n9\t#include <omp.h>\n10\t\n11\t#ifdef __linux__\n12\t#include <sched.h>\n13\t#include <unistd.h>\n14\t#endif\n15\t\n16\t#if defined(__x86_64__) || defined(__i386__)\n17\t#include <immintrin.h>\n18\t#endif\n19\t\n20\t#include \"../common/results_output.hpp\"\n21\t\n22\t// Pin every OpenMP thread to a distinct CPU, spread over the available cores.\n23\t// This kernel is memory bound, so keeping threads (and thus the pages they\n24\t// first touch) on a fixed NUMA node is worth several times the performance.\n25\t// Only done when the user did not request an affinity policy: libgomp's\n26\t// default is OMP_PROC_BIND=false with an empty place list, in which case the\n27\t// proc_bind clauses below have no effect and threads are free to migrate.\n28\tstatic void bindThreadsToCores() {\n29\t#ifdef __linux__\n30\t    if (omp_get_proc_bind() != omp_proc_bind_false) return;\n31\t\n32\t    cpu_set_t allowed;\n33\t    CPU_ZERO(&allowed);\n34\t    if (sched_getaffinity(0, sizeof(allowed), &allowed) != 0) return;\n35\t\n36\t    std::vector<int> cpus;\n37\t    for (int i = 0; i < CPU_SETSIZE; ++i) {\n38\t        if (CPU_ISSET(i, &allowed)) cpus.push_back(i);\n39\t    }\n40\t    if (cpus.empty()) return;\n41\t    const int ncpus = static_cast<int>(cpus.size());\n42\t\n43\t#pragma omp parallel\n44\t    {\n45\t        const int tid = omp_get_thread_num();\n46\t        const int nthreads = omp_get_num_threads();\n47\t        // Spread: with fewer threads than CPUs, stride over the CPU list so\n48\t        // both sockets are used before any SMT sibling pair is doubled up.\n49\t        const int slot = (nthreads <= ncpus)\n50\t                             ? static_cast<int>(static_cast<long long>(tid) * ncpus / nthreads)\n51\t                             : (tid % ncpus);\n52\t        cpu_set_t set;\n53\t        CPU_ZERO(&set);\n54\t        CPU_SET(cpus[slot], &set);\n55\t        sched_setaffinity(0, sizeof(set), &set);\n56\t    }\n57\t#endif\n58\t}\n59\t\n60\t// 3D index calculation\n61\tinline constexpr size_t idx3(const size_t x, const size_t y, const size_t z, const size_t nx, const size_t ny) noexcept {\n62\t    return z * (nx * ny) + y * nx + x;\n63\t}\n64\t\n65\t// One row (fixed y, z) of the 7-point Laplacian stencil with clamped boundary\n66\t// conditions in x. `cc` is the row itself, the other four pointers are the\n67\t// already clamped neighbour rows in y and z. `body(index, value, laplacian)`\n68\t// produces the output value; `base` is the linear index of the row start.\n69\ttemplate <typename Body>\n70\tstatic inline void stencilRow(const double* __restrict cc, const double* __restrict cyp,\n71\t                              const double* __restrict cyn, const double* __restrict czp,\n72\t                              const double* __restrict czn, double* __restrict o,\n73\t                              const size_t nx, const size_t base,\n74\t                              const double dx2, const double dy2, const double dz2,\n75\t                              Body body) {\n76\t    // x = 0 (clamped low edge)\n77\t    {\n78\t        const double c0 = cc[0];\n79\t        const double lap = (cc[(nx > 1) ? i < CPU_SETSIZE; ++i) {\n        if (CPU_ISSET(i, &allowed)) cpus.push_back(i);\n    }\n    if (cpus.empty()) return;\n    const int ncpus = static_cast<int>(cpus.size());\n\n#pragma omp parallel\n    {\n        const int tid = omp_get_thread_num();\n        const int nthreads = omp_get_num_threads();\n        // Spread: with fewer threads than CPUs, stride over the CPU list so\n        // both sockets are used before any SMT sibling pair is doubled up.\n        const int slot = (nthreads <= ncpus)\n                             ? static_cast<int>(static_cast<long long>(tid) * ncpus / nthreads)\n                             : (tid % ncpus);\n        cpu_set_t set;\n        CPU_ZERO(&set);\n        CPU_SET(cpus[slot], &set);\n        sched_setaffinity(0, sizeof(set), &set);\n    }\n#endif\n}\n\n// 3D index calculation\ninline constexpr size_t idx3(const size_t x, const size_t y, const size_t z, const size_t nx, const size_t ny) noexcept {\n    return z * (nx * ny) + y * nx + x;\n}\n\n// One row (fixed y, z) of the 7-point Laplacian stencil with clamped boundary\n// conditions in x. `cc` is the row itself, the other four pointers are the\n// already clamped neighbour rows in y and z. `body(index, value, laplacian)`\n// produces the output value; Compared with two full sweeps this removes all\n// memory traffic for mu (write + read) and the second read of the\n// concentration field, at the cost of recomputing mu on a one row wide halo\n// around each tile. The arithmetic per cell is unchanged, so results are\n// bit-identical to the non-fused path.\n//\n// This is an orphaned worksharing construct: call it from a parallel region.\nstatic inline void fusedStep(const double* __restrict cold, double* __restrict cnew,\n                             double* __restrict ring, const size_t ringRows,\n                             double* __restrict scratch,\n                             const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,\n                             const double dx2, const double dy2, const double dz2,\n                             const double gamma, const double e_AA, const double e_BB, const double e_AB,\n                             const double D, const double dt, const Tiling& t) {\n    const size_t slab = nx * ny;\n    const size_t ringPlane = ringRows * nx;\n\n#pragma omp for schedule(static) nowait\n    for (size_t tile = 0; tile < t.ntiles; ++tile) {\n        size_t y0, y1, z0, z1;\n        tileBounds(t, tile, ny, nz, y0, y1, z0, z1);\n        if (y0 >= y1 || z0 >= z1) continue;\n\n        // rows of mu this tile needs (its own rows plus one halo row per side)\n        const size_t ylo = (y0 > 0) ? y0 - 1 : 0;\n        const size_t yhi = (y1 < ny) ? y1 + 1 : ny;\n\n        const size_t pstart = (z0 > 0) ? z0 - 1 : 0;\n        const size_t pend = (z1 < nz) ? z1 : nz - 1;  // inclusive\n\n        for (size_t p = pstart; p <= pend; ++p) {\n            // --- produce mu for plane p into ring slot p % 3 ---\n            double* __restrict dst = ring + (p % 3) * ringPlane;\n            const size_t zp = ((p < nz - 1) ?
